{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-playbook\n\n# Frontend Playbook\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"I'll use `yarn chain`.\"** Wrong. `yarn chain` gives you an empty local chain with no protocols, no tokens, no state. `yarn fork --network base` gives you a copy of real Base with Uniswap, Aave, USDC, real whale balances — everything (verified addresses: `addresses/SKILL.md`). Always fork.\n\n**\"I deployed to IPFS and it works.\"** Did the CID change? If not, you deployed stale output. Did routes work? Without `trailingSlash: true`, every route except `/` returns 404. Did you check the OG image? Without `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL`, it points to `localhost:3000`.\n\n**\"I'll set up the project manually.\"** Don't. `npx create-eth@latest` handles everything — Foundry, Next.js, RainbowKit, scaffold hooks. Never run `forge init` or create Next.js projects from scratch.\n\n---\n\n## Fork Mode Setup\n\n### Why Fork, Not Chain\n\n```\nyarn chain (WRONG)              yarn fork --network base (CORRECT)\n└─ Empty local chain            └─ Fork of real Base mainnet\n└─ No protocols                 └─ Uniswap, Aave, etc. available\n└─ No tokens                    └─ Real USDC, WETH exist\n└─ Testing in isolation         └─ Test against REAL state\n```\n\n### Setup\n\n```bash\nnpx create-eth@latest          # Select: foundry, target chain, name\ncd <project-name>\nyarn install\nyarn fork --network base       # Terminal 1: fork of real Base\nyarn deploy                    # Terminal 2: deploy contracts to fork\nyarn start                     # Terminal 3: Next.js frontend\n```\n\n### Critical: Chain ID Gotcha\n\n**When using fork mode, the frontend target network MUST be `chains.foundry` (chain ID 31337), NOT the chain you're forking.**\n\nThe fork runs locally on Anvil with chain ID 31337. Even if you're forking Base:\n\n```typescript\n// scaffold.config.ts during development\ntargetNetworks: [chains.foundry],  // ✅ NOT chains.base!\n```\n\nOnly switch to `chains.base` when deploying contracts to the REAL network.\n\n### Enable Block Mining\n\n```bash\n# In a new terminal — REQUIRED for time-dependent logic\ncast rpc anvil_setIntervalMining 1\n```\n\nWithout this, `block.timestamp` stays FROZEN. Any contract logic using timestamps (deadlines, expiry, vesting) will break silently.\n\n**Make it permanent** by editing `packages/foundry/package.json` to add `--block-time 1` to the fork script.\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to IPFS (Recommended)\n\nIPFS is the recommended deploy path for SE2. Avoids Vercel's memory limits entirely. Produces a fully decentralized static site.\n\n### Full Build Command\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out  # ALWAYS clean first\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://yourapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Upload to BuidlGuidl IPFS\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# Save the CID!\n```\n\n### Node 25+ localStorage Polyfill (REQUIRED)\n\nNode.js 25+ ships a built-in `localStorage` object that's MISSING standard WebStorage API methods (`getItem`, `setItem`). This breaks `next-themes`, RainbowKit, and any library that calls `localStorage.getItem()` during static page generation.\n\n**Error you'll see:**\n```\nTypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function\nError occurred prerendering page \"/_not-found\"\n```\n\n**The fix:** Create `polyfill-localstorage.cjs` in `packages/nextjs/`:\n```javascript\nif (typeof globalThis.localStorage !== \"undefined\" &&\n    typeof globalThis.localStorage.getItem !== \"function\") {\n  const store = new Map();\n  globalThis.localStorage = {\n    getItem: (key) => store.get(key) ?? null,\n    setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, String(value)),\n    removeItem: (key) => store.delete(key),\n    clear: () => store.clear(),\n    key: (index) => [...store.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    get length() { return store.size; },\n  };\n}\n```\n\n**Why `--require` and not `instrumentation.ts`?** Next.js spawns a separate build worker process for prerendering. `--require` injects into EVERY Node process (including workers). `next.config.ts` polyfill only runs in the main process. `instrumentation.ts` doesn't run in the build worker. Only `--require` works.\n\n### IPFS Routing — Why Routes Break\n\nIPFS gateways serve static files. No server handles routing. Three things MUST be true:\n\n**1. `output: \"export\"` in next.config.ts** — generates static HTML files.\n\n**2. `trailingSlash: true` (CRITICAL)** — This is the #1 reason routes break:\n- `trailingSlash: false` (default) → generates `debug.html`\n- `trailingSlash: true` → generates `debug/index.html`\n- IPFS gateways resolve directories to `index.html` automatically, but NOT bare filenames\n- Without trailing slash: `/debug` → 404 ❌\n- With trailing slash: `/debug` → `debug/` → `debug/index.html` ✅\n\n**3. Pages must survive static prerendering** — any page that crashes during `yarn build` (browser APIs at import time, localStorage) gets skipped silently → 404 on IPFS.\n\n**The complete IPFS-safe next.config.ts pattern:**\n```typescript\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = { unoptimized: true };\n}\n```\n\n**SE2's block explorer pages** use `localStorage` at import time and crash during static export. Rename `app/blockexplorer` to `app/_blockexplorer-disabled` if not needed.\n\n### Stale Build Detection\n\n**The #1 IPFS footgun:** You edit code, then deploy the OLD build.\n\n```bash\n# MANDATORY after ANY code change:\nrm -rf .next out                     # 1. Delete old artifacts\n# ... run full build command ...     # 2. Rebuild from scratch\ngrep -l \"YOUR_STRING\" out/_next/static/chunks/app/*.js  # 3. Verify changes present\n\n# Timestamp check:\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx           # Source modified time\nstat -f '%Sm' out/                   # Build output time\n# Source NEWER than out/ = STALE BUILD. Rebuild first!\n```\n\n**The CID is proof:** If the IPFS CID didn't change after a deploy, you deployed the same content. A real code change ALWAYS produces a new CID.\n\n### Verify Routes After Deploy\n\n```bash\nls out/*/index.html                  # Each route has a directory + index.html\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://GATEWAY/ipfs/CID/debug/\"\n# Should return 200, not 404\n```\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to Vercel (Alternative)\n\nSE2 is a monorepo — Vercel needs special configuration.\n\n### Configuration\n\n1. **Root Directory:** `packages/nextjs`\n2. **Install Command:** `cd ../.. && yarn install`\n3. **Build Command:** leave default (`next build`)\n4. **Output Directory:** leave default (`.next`)\n\n```bash\n# Via API:\ncurl -X PATCH \"https://api.vercel.com/v9/projects/PROJECT_ID\" \\\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer $VERCEL_TOKEN\" \\\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\n  -d '{\"rootDirectory\": \"packages/nextjs\", \"installCommand\": \"cd ../.. && yarn install\"}'\n```\n\n### Common Failures\n\n| Error | Cause | Fix |\n|-------|-------|-----|\n| \"No Next.js version detected\" | Root Directory not set | Set to `packages/nextjs` |\n| \"cd packages/nextjs: No such file\" | Build command has `cd` | Clear it — root dir handles this |\n| OOM / exit code 129 | SE2 monorepo exceeds 8GB | Use IPFS instead, or `vercel --prebuilt` |\n\n### Decision Tree\n\n```\nWant to deploy SE2?\n├─ IPFS (recommended) → yarn ipfs / manual build + upload\n│   └─ Fully decentralized, no memory limits, works with ENS\n├─ Vercel → Set rootDirectory + installCommand\n│   └─ Fast CDN, but centralized. May OOM on large projects\n└─ vercel --prebuilt → Build locally, push artifacts to Vercel\n    └─ Best of both: local build power + Vercel CDN\n```\n\n---\n\n## ENS Subdomain Setup\n\nTwo mainnet transactions to point an ENS subdomain at your IPFS deployment.\n\n### Transaction 1: Create Subdomain (new apps only)\n\n1. Open `https://app.ens.domains/yourname.eth`\n2. Go to \"Subnames\" tab → \"New subname\"\n3. Enter the label (e.g. `myapp`) → Next → Skip profile → Open Wallet → Confirm\n4. If gas is stuck: switch MetaMask to Ethereum → Activity tab → \"Speed up\"\n\n### Transaction 2: Set IPFS Content Hash\n\n1. Navigate to `https://app.ens.domains/myapp.yourname.eth`\n2. \"Records\" tab → \"Edit Records\" → \"Other\" tab\n3. Paste in Content Hash field: `ipfs://<CID>`\n4. Save → Open Wallet → Confirm in MetaMask\n\nFor **updates** to an existing app: skip Tx 1, only do Tx 2.\n\n### Verify\n\n```bash\n# 1. Onchain content hash matches\nRESOLVER=$(cast call 0x00000000000C2e074eC69A0dFb2997BA6C7d2e1e \\\n  \"resolver(bytes32)(address)\" $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) \\\n  --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com)\ncast call $RESOLVER \"contenthash(bytes32)(bytes)\" \\\n  $(cast namehash myapp.yourname.eth) --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com\n\n# 2. Gateway responds (may take 5-15 min for cache)\ncurl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\"\n\n# 3. OG metadata correct (not localhost)\ncurl -s -L \"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" | grep 'og:image'\n```\n\n**Use `.eth.link` NOT `.eth.limo`** — `.eth.link` works better on mobile.\n\n---\n\n## Go to Production — Complete Checklist\n\nWhen the user says \"ship it\", follow this EXACT sequence.\n\n### Step 1: Final Code Review 🤖\n- All feedback incorporated\n- No duplicate h1, no raw addresses, no shared isLoading\n- `scaffold.config.ts` has `rpcOverrides` and `pollingInterval: 3000`\n\n### Step 2: Choose Domain 👤\nAsk: *\"What subdomain do you want? e.g. `myapp.yourname.eth` → `myapp.yourname.eth.link`\"*\n\n### Step 3: Generate OG Image + Fix Metadata 🤖\n- Create 1200×630 PNG (`public/thumbnail.png`) — NOT the stock SE2 thumbnail\n- Set `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` to the live domain\n- Verify `og:image` will resolve to an absolute production URL\n\n### Step 4: Clean Build + IPFS Deploy 🤖\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs && rm -rf .next out\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://myapp.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n\n# Verify before uploading:\nls out/*/index.html                        # Routes exist\ngrep 'og:image' out/index.html             # Not localhost\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx                 # Source older than out/\nstat -f '%Sm' out/\n\nyarn bgipfs upload out                     # Save the CID\n```\n\n### Step 5: Share for Approval 👤\nSend: *\"Build ready for review: `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>`\"*\n**Wait for approval before touching ENS.**\n\n### Step 6: Set ENS 🤖\nCreate subdomain (if new) + set IPFS content hash. Two mainnet transactions.\n\n### Step 7: Verify 🤖\n- Content hash matches onchain\n- `.eth.link` gateway responds with 200\n- OG image loads correctly\n- Routes work (`/debug/`, etc.)\n\n### Step 8: Report 👤\n*\"Live at `https://myapp.yourname.eth.link` — ENS content hash confirmed onchain, unfurl metadata set.\"*\n\n---\n\n## Build Verification Process\n\nA build is NOT done when the code compiles. It's done when you've tested it like a real user.\n\n### Phase 1: Code QA (Automated)\n- Scan `.tsx` files for raw address strings (should use `<Address/>`)\n- Scan for shared `isLoading` state across multiple buttons\n- Scan for missing `disabled` props on transaction buttons\n- Verify RPC config and polling interval\n- Verify OG metadata with absolute URLs\n- Verify no public RPCs in any file\n\n### Phase 2: Smart Contract Testing\n```bash\nforge test                    # All tests pass\nforge test --fuzz-runs 10000  # Fuzz testing\n```\nTest edge cases: zero amounts, max amounts, unauthorized callers, reentrancy attempts.\n\n### Phase 3: Browser Testing (THE REAL TEST)\n\nOpen the app and do a FULL walkthrough:\n\n1. **Load the app** — does it render correctly?\n2. **Check page title** — is it correct, not \"Scaffold-ETH 2\"?\n3. **Connect wallet** — does the connect flow work?\n4. **Wrong network** — connect on wrong chain, verify \"Switch to Base\" appears\n5. **Switch network** — click the switch button, verify it works\n6. **Approve flow** — verify approve button shows, click it, wait for tx, verify action button appears\n7. **Main action** — click primary action, verify loader, wait for tx, verify state updates\n8. **Error handling** — reject a transaction in wallet, verify UI recovers\n9. **Address displays** — all addresses showing ENS/blockies, not raw hex?\n10. **Share URL** — check OG unfurl (image, title, description)\n\n### Phase 4: QA Sub-Agent (Complex Builds)\nFor bigger projects, spawn a sub-agent with fresh context. Give it the repo path and deployed URL. It reads all code against the UX rules, opens a browser, clicks through independently, and reports issues.\n\n---\n\n## Don't Do These\n\n- ❌ `yarn chain` — use `yarn fork --network <chain>`\n- ❌ `forge init` — use `npx create-eth@latest`\n- ❌ Manual Next.js setup — SE2 handles it\n- ❌ Manual wallet connection — SE2 has RainbowKit pre-configured\n- ❌ Edit `deployedContracts.ts` — it's auto-generated by `yarn deploy`\n- ❌ Hardcode API keys in `scaffold.config.ts` — use `.env.local`\n- ❌ Use `mainnet.base.org` in production — use Alchemy or similar\n\n---\n\n## Resources\n\n- **SE2 Docs:** https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **UI Components:** https://ui.scaffoldeth.io/\n- **SpeedRun Ethereum:** https://speedrunethereum.com/\n- **ETH Tech Tree:** https://www.ethtechtree.com\n- **BuidlGuidl IPFS:** https://upload.bgipfs.com\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"78059705-c215-47a0-9eb8-3d761c67b488","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:09:17.292Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VFw5cCNrm8x2noZf7gX5Lq","type":"tool_result","content":"===skill dir===\n.claude/skills/frontend-playbook/SKILL.md\n===foundry Makefile fork target===\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n===foundry .env.example===\n# Template for foundry environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# We provide default values so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY is used while deploying contract.\n# On anvil chain the value of it can be empty since we use the prefunded account\n# which comes with anvil chain to deploy contract.\n# NOTE: You don't need to manually change the value of DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY, it should\n# be auto filled when run `yarn generate`.\n\n# Alchemy rpc URL is used while deploying the contracts to some testnets/mainnets, checkout `foundry.toml` for it's use.\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\n# Etherscan API key is used to verify the contract on etherscan.\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=DNXJA8RX2Q3VZ4URQIWP7Z68CJXQZSC6AW\n# Default account for localhost / use \"scaffold-eth-custom\" if you wish to use a generated account or imported account\nLOCALHOST_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT=scaffold-eth-default\n===foundry.toml===\n[profile.default]\nsrc = 'contracts'\nout = 'out'\nlibs = ['lib', 'node_modules']\nfs_permissions = [{ access = \"read-write\", path = \"./\"}]\nextra_output = [\"evm.bytecode.opcodes\"]\noptimizer = true\noptimizer_runs = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JvGd6P8cMfB4VRQjUjz4WP","type":"tool_result","content":"===scaffold.config===\nimport * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n\n\n\nexport type BaseConfig = {\n  targetNetworks: readonly chains.Chain[];\n  pollingInterval: number;\n  alchemyApiKey: string;\n  rpcOverrides?: Record<number, string>;\n  walletConnectProjectId: string;\n  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n};\n\nexport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";\n\nconst scaffoldConfig = {\n  // The networks on which your DApp is live\n  targetNetworks: [\n    chains.foundry\n  ],\n  // The interval at which your front-end polls the RPC servers for new data (it has no effect if you only target the local network (default is 4000))\n  pollingInterval: 3000,\n  // This is ours Alchemy's default API key.\n  // You can get your own at https://dashboard.alchemyapi.io\n  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n  alchemyApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY || DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY,\n  // If you want to use a different RPC for a specific network, you can add it here.\n  // The key is the chain ID, and the value is the HTTP RPC URL\n  rpcOverrides: {\n    // Example:\n    // [chains.mainnet.id]: \"https://mainnet.rpc.buidlguidl.com\",\n  },\n  // This is ours WalletConnect's default project ID.\n  // You can get your own at https://cloud.walletconnect.com\n  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n  walletConnectProjectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID || '3a8170812b534d0ff9d794f19a901d64',\n  // Configure Burner Wallet visibility:\n  // - \"localNetworksOnly\": only show when all target networks are local (hardhat/anvil)\n  // - \"allNetworks\": show on any configured target networks\n  // - \"disabled\": completely disable\n  burnerWalletMode: 'localNetworksOnly'\n} as const satisfies ScaffoldConfig;\n\nexport default scaffoldConfig;===next.config===\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\"\n  }\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\n\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;===nextjs app tree===\npackages/nextjs/app\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer\npackages/nextjs/app/debug\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/transaction\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/_components\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/address\npackages/nextjs/app/debug/_components\n===nextjs package.json scripts===\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"check-types\": \"tsc --noEmit --incremental\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"format\": \"prettier --write . '!(node_modules|.next)/**/*'\",\n    \"ipfs\": \"NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn build && yarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com && CID=$(yarn bgipfs upload out | grep -o 'CID: [^ ]*' | cut -d' ' -f2) && [ ! -z \\\"$CID\\\" ] && echo '🚀 Upload complete! Your site is now available at: https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/'$CID || echo '❌ Upload failed'\",\n    \"lint\": \"yarn eslint .\",\n    \"serve\": \"next start\",\n    \"start\": \"next dev\",\n    \"vercel\": \"vercel --build-env YARN_ENABLE_IMMUTABLE_INSTALLS=false --build-env ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_COREPACK=1 --build-env VERCEL_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1\",\n    \"vercel:login\": \"vercel login\",\n    \"vercel:yolo\": \"vercel --build-env YARN_ENABLE_IMMUTABLE_INSTALLS=false --build-env ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_COREPACK=1 --build-env NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true --build-env VERCEL_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"@heroicons/react\": \"~2.2.0\",\n    \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\": \"2.2.9\",\n    \"@react-native-async-storage/async-storage\": \"~2.2.0\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/components\": \"^0.1.12\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/debug-contracts\": \"^0.1.11\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/hooks\": \"^0.1.8\",\n    \"@tanstack/react-query\": \"~5.59.15\",\n    \"blo\": \"~2.0.0\",\n    \"burner-connector\": \"0.0.20\",\n    \"daisyui\": \"5.5.19\",\n    \"kubo-rpc-client\": \"~6.1.0\",\n    \"next\": \"~16.2.4\",\n===node version===\nv25.9.0\n===YourContract===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"3f75f26f-b88e-4e37-bd61-5898ecf0cce4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:09:41.328Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===scaffold.config===\nimport \"localNetworksOnly\": only show when all target networks are local (hardhat/anvil)\n  // - \"allNetworks\": show on any configured target networks\n  // - \"disabled\": completely disable\n  burnerWalletMode: 'localNetworksOnly'\n} as const satisfies ScaffoldConfig;\n\nexport default scaffoldConfig;===next.config===\nimport type { NextConfig } from \"next\";\n\n\nconst nextConfig: NextConfig = {\n  reactStrictMode: true,\n  devIndicators: false,\n  typescript: {\n    ignoreBuildErrors: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR === \"true\"\n  }\n};\n\nconst isIpfs = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD === \"true\";\n\nif (isIpfs) {\n  nextConfig.output = \"export\";\n  nextConfig.trailingSlash = true;\n  nextConfig.images = {\n    unoptimized: true,\n  };\n}\n\n\n\nmodule.exports = nextConfig;===nextjs app tree===\npackages/nextjs/app\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer\npackages/nextjs/app/debug\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/transaction\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/_components\npackages/nextjs/app/blockexplorer/address\npackages/nextjs/app/debug/_components\n===nextjs package.json scripts===\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"yarn lint && next build\",\n    \"check-types\": \"tsc --noEmit --incremental\",\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"format\": \"prettier --write . '!(node_modules|.next)/**/*'\",\n    \"ipfs\": \"NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn build && yarn bgipfs upload config init -u https://upload.bgipfs.com && CID=$(yarn bgipfs upload out | grep -o 'CID: [^ ]*' | cut -d' ' -f2) && [ ! -z \\\"$CID\\\" ] && echo '🚀 Upload complete! Your site is now available at: https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/'$CID || echo '❌ Upload failed'\",\n    \"lint\": \"yarn eslint .\",\n    \"serve\": \"next start\",\n    \"start\": \"next dev\",\n    \"vercel\": \"vercel --build-env YARN_ENABLE_IMMUTABLE_INSTALLS=false --build-env ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_COREPACK=1 --build-env VERCEL_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1\",\n    \"vercel:login\": \"vercel login\",\n    \"vercel:yolo\": \"vercel --build-env YARN_ENABLE_IMMUTABLE_INSTALLS=false --build-env ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_COREPACK=1 --build-env NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true --build-env VERCEL_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"@heroicons/react\": \"~2.2.0\",\n    \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\": \"2.2.9\",\n    \"@react-native-async-storage/async-storage\": \"~2.2.0\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/components\": \"^0.1.12\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/debug-contracts\": \"^0.1.11\",\n    \"@scaffold-ui/hooks\": \"^0.1.8\",\n    \"@tanstack/react-query\": \"~5.59.15\",\n    \"blo\": \"~2.0.0\",\n    \"burner-connector\": \"0.0.20\",\n    \"daisyui\": \"5.5.19\",\n    \"kubo-rpc-client\": \"~6.1.0\",\n    \"next\": \"~16.2.4\",\n===node version===\nv25.9.0\n===YourContract===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"forge-std/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n  // State Variables\n  address public immutable owner;\n  string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n  bool public premium = false;\n  uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n  mapping(address => uint256) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n  // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n  event GreetingChange(\n    address indexed greetingSetter,\n    string newGreeting,\n    bool premium,\n    uint256 value\n  );\n\n  // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n  // Check packages/foundry/deploy/Deploy.s.sol\n  constructor(address _owner) {\n    owner = _owner;\n  }\n\n  // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n  // Check the withdraw() function\n  modifier isOwner() {\n    // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n    require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n    _;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n   *\n   * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n   */\n  function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n    // Print data to the anvil chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n\n    console.logString(\"Setting new greeting\");\n    console.logString(_newGreeting);\n\n    greeting = _newGreeting;\n    totalCounter += 1;\n    userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n    // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n    if (msg.value > 0) {\n      premium = true;\n    } else {\n      premium = false;\n    }\n\n    // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n    emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n   * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n   */\n  function withdraw() public isOwner {\n    (bool success,) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n    require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n   */\n  receive() external payable { }\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018gXZpfAVHSNBC1ztPXDoWe","type":"tool_result","content":"===DeployHelpers===\n//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Script, console } from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport { Vm } from \"forge-std/Vm.sol\";\n\ncontract ScaffoldETHDeploy is Script {\n    error InvalidChain();\n    error DeployerHasNoBalance();\n    error InvalidPrivateKey(string);\n\n    event AnvilSetBalance(address account, uint256 amount);\n    event FailedAnvilRequest();\n\n    struct Deployment {\n        string name;\n        address addr;\n    }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address deployer;\n\n    /// @notice Use this modifier on your run() function on your deploy scripts\n    modifier ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner() {\n        deployer = _startBroadcast();\n        if (deployer == address(0)) {\n            revert InvalidPrivateKey(\"Invalid private key\");\n        }\n        _;\n        _stopBroadcast();\n        exportDeployments();\n    }\n\n    function _startBroadcast() internal returns (address) {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        (, address _deployer,) = vm.readCallers();\n\n        if (block.chainid == 31337 && _deployer.balance == 0) {\n            try vm.deal(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE) {\n                emit AnvilSetBalance(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE);\n            } catch {\n                emit FailedAnvilRequest();\n            }\n        }\n        return _deployer;\n    }\n\n    function _stopBroadcast() internal {\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n    }\n\n    function exportDeployments() internal {\n        // fetch already existing contracts\n        root = vm.projectRoot();\n        path = string.concat(root, \"/deployments/\");\n        string memory chainIdStr = vm.toString(block.chainid);\n        path = string.concat(path, string.concat(chainIdStr, \".json\"));\n\n        string memory jsonWrite;\n\n        uint256 len = deployments.length;\n\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; i++) {\n            vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, vm.toString(deployments[i].addr), deployments[i].name);\n        }\n\n        string memory chainName;\n\n        try vm.getChain(block.chainid) returns (Vm.Chain memory chain) {\n            chainName = chain.name;\n        } catch {\n            chainName = findChainName();\n        }\n        jsonWrite = vm.serializeString(jsonWrite, \"networkName\", chainName);\n        vm.writeJson(jsonWrite, path);\n===getMetadata===\npackages/nextjs/utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata.ts\n\nimport type { Metadata } from \"next\";\n\n\nconst baseUrl = process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL\n  ? `https://${process.env.VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL}`\n  : `http://localhost:${process.env.PORT || 3000}`;\nconst titleTemplate = \"%s | Scaffold-ETH 2\";\n\nexport const getMetadata = ({\n  title,\n  description,\n  imageRelativePath = \"/thumbnail.jpg\",\n}: {\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  imageRelativePath?: string;\n}): Metadata => {\n  const imageUrl = `${baseUrl}${imageRelativePath}`;\n\n  return {\n  metadataBase: new URL(baseUrl),\n  title: {\n    default: title,\n    template: titleTemplate\n  },\n  description: description,\n  openGraph: {\n    title: {\n      default: title,\n      template: titleTemplate\n    },\n    description: description,\n    images: [\n      {\n        url: imageUrl\n      }\n    ]\n  },\n  twitter: {\n    title: {\n      default: title,\n      template: titleTemplate\n    },\n    description: description,\n    images: [\n      imageUrl\n    ]\n  },\n  icons: {\n    icon: [\n      {\n        url: '/favicon.png',\n        sizes: '32x32',\n        type: 'image/png'\n      }\n    ]\n  }\n};\n}===layout===\n\nimport \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit/styles.css\";\nimport \"@scaffold-ui/components/styles.css\";\nimport { ScaffoldEthAppWithProviders } from \"~~/components/ScaffoldEthAppWithProviders\";\nimport { ThemeProvider } from \"~~/components/ThemeProvider\";\nimport \"~~/styles/globals.css\";\nimport { getMetadata } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/getMetadata\";\n\n\nexport const metadata = getMetadata({\n  title: 'Scaffold-ETH 2 App',\n  description: 'Built with 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2'\n});\n\nconst ScaffoldEthApp = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {\n  return (\n    <html suppressHydrationWarning className={``}>\n      <body>\n        <ThemeProvider enableSystem>\n          <ScaffoldEthAppWithProviders>{children}</ScaffoldEthAppWithProviders>\n        </ThemeProvider>\n      </body>\n    </html>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ScaffoldEthApp;===header nav===\n15:  label: string;\n16:  href: string;\n20:export const menuLinks: HeaderMenuLink[] = [\n22: InvalidChain();\n    error DeployerHasNoBalance();\n    error InvalidPrivateKey(string);\n\n    event AnvilSetBalance(address account, uint256 amount);\n    event FailedAnvilRequest();\n\n    struct Deployment {\n        string name;\n        address addr;\n    }\n\n    string root;\n    string path;\n    Deployment[] public deployments;\n    uint256 constant ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE = 10000 ether;\n\n    /// @notice The deployer address for every run\n    address deployer;\n\n    /// @notice Use this modifier on your run() function on your deploy scripts\n    modifier ScaffoldEthDeployerRunner() {\n        deployer = _startBroadcast();\n        if (deployer == address(0)) {\n            revert InvalidPrivateKey(\"Invalid private key\");\n        }\n        _;\n        _stopBroadcast();\n        exportDeployments();\n    }\n\n    function _startBroadcast() internal returns (address) {\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        (, address _deployer,) = vm.readCallers();\n\n        if (block.chainid == 31337 && _deployer.balance == 0) {\n            try vm.deal(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE) {\n                emit AnvilSetBalance(_deployer, ANVIL_BASE_BALANCE);\n            } catch {\n                emit FailedAnvilRequest();\n            }\n        }\n        return _deployer;\n    }\n\n    function _stopBroadcast() internal {\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n    }\n\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMQwyatUEGAZdTJaaA5D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01J3yXdvGipqquk2bCbDaCuY","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A USDC tip jar. Anyone can send a USDC tip with a short message; the\n *         owner can withdraw the collected balance. Every tip is emitted as an\n *         event so the frontend can render a live feed.\n * @dev Tips are pulled with `transferFrom`, so the tipper must `approve` this\n *      contract for at least `amount` first. USDC is a fixed 6-decimal token.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The token accepted as tips (USDC on Base: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips received.\n    uint256 public tipCount;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative amount tipped, in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Emitted on every tip. Indexed by tipper so the feed can filter by sender.\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n\n    /// @notice Emitted when the owner withdraws the collected balance.\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0) && _owner != address(0), \"zero address\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals; 1 USDC = 1_000_000).\n     * @param message Short public message shown in the feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Interactions: pull the tip. SafeERC20 reverts if the transfer fails,\n        // so state below only updates on a confirmed transfer.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        // Effects: counters are stats only, so post-transfer ordering is safe here.\n        tipCount += 1;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the full collected USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar, awaiting withdrawal.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1342,"cache_read_input_tokens":47637,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1342},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"9e4d2f60-e326-4d2a-b208-7f272c611668","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:12:42.524Z","request_id":"req_011CdMQwxJzcAUjRjHYB74Bx"}

Anyone can send a USDC tip with a short message; the\n *         owner can withdraw the collected balance. Every tip is emitted as an\n *         event so the frontend can render a live feed.\n * @dev Tips are pulled with `transferFrom`, so the tipper must `approve` this\n *      contract for at least `amount` first. USDC is a fixed 6-decimal token.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The token accepted as tips (USDC on Base: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips received.\n    uint256 public tipCount;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative amount tipped, in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Emitted on every tip. Indexed by tipper so the feed can filter by sender.\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n\n    /// @notice Emitted when the owner withdraws the collected balance.\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0) && _owner != address(0), \"zero address\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals; 1 USDC = 1_000_000).\n     * @param message Short public message shown in the feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Interactions: pull the tip. SafeERC20 reverts if the transfer fails,\n        // so state below only updates on a confirmed transfer.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        // Effects: counters are stats only, so post-transfer ordering is safe here.\n        tipCount += 1;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the full collected USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar, awaiting withdrawal.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMR1LTaveN7fE2t7zxB1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QNTjbq8EQBUVfXcCHoY2Tc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.19;\n\nimport { Test } from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport { ERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\nimport { TipJar } from \"../contracts/TipJar.sol\";\n\n/// @dev Minimal 6-decimal token that mimics USDC for unit tests (no fork needed).\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\") { }\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE_USDC = 1e6;\n\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc), owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1000 * ONE_USDC);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1000 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_Constructor() public view {\n        assertEq(address(jar.usdc()), address(usdc));\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), owner);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 0);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroAddresses() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(\"zero address\");\n        new TipJar(address(0), owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(\"zero address\");\n        new TipJar(address(usdc), address(0));\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_MovesUsdcAndUpdatesState() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit NewTip(alice, 5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(5 * ONE_USDC, \"gm\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 995 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 5 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_MultipleTippersAccumulate() public {\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE_USDC, \"one\");\n        _tip(bob, 7 * ONE_USDC, \"two\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 2);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(ONE_USDC, \"no allowance\");\n    }\n\n    function test_Tip_RevertsWhenBalanceTooLow() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        jar.tip(2000 * ONE_USDC, \"too much\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_OnlyOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 4 * ONE_USDC, \"hi\");\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_RevertsWhenEmpty() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NothingToWithdraw.selector);\n        jar.withdraw();\n    }\n\n    function test_Withdraw_TransfersFullBalanceToOwner() public {\n        _tip(alice, 4 * ONE_USDC, \"hi\");\n        _tip(bob, 6 * ONE_USDC, \"yo\");\n\n        vm.expectEmit(true, false, false, true);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 10 * ONE_USDC);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function testFuzz_Tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) public {\n        amount = bound(amount, 1, 1000 * ONE_USDC);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), amount);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), amount);\n        assertEq(jar.tipCount(), 1);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address from, uint256 amount, string memory message) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(from);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, message);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":367,"cache_read_input_tokens":52624,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":367},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"b66e9d78-d796-4229-95b0-847bda55c49c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:13:33.263Z","request_id":"req_011CdMR1KQaucLT2YZXueHgo"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WnJznfvFdeXmx4HaHLJhPu","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"3ba36def-43e6-42a3-8387-8f5b626a59c6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:13:43.601Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol","oldString":"        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);","newString":"        uint256 collected = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (collected == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, collected);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, collected);","originalFile":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\nimport { IERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport { SafeERC20 } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @notice A USDC tip jar. Anyone can send a USDC tip with a short message; the\n *         owner can withdraw the collected balance. Every tip is emitted as an\n *         event so the frontend can render a live feed.\n * @dev Tips are pulled with `transferFrom`, so the tipper must `approve` this\n *      contract for at least `amount` first. USDC is a fixed 6-decimal token.\n */\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice The token accepted as tips (USDC on Base: 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public immutable owner;\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips received.\n    uint256 public tipCount;\n\n    /// @notice Cumulative amount tipped, in USDC's smallest unit (6 decimals).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Emitted on every tip. Indexed by tipper so the feed can filter by sender.\n    event NewTip(address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp);\n\n    /// @notice Emitted when the owner withdraws the collected balance.\n    event Withdraw(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error NothingToWithdraw();\n\n    constructor(address _usdc, address _owner) {\n        require(_usdc != address(0) && _owner != address(0), \"zero address\");\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n     * @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (6 decimals; 1 USDC = 1_000_000).\n     * @param message Short public message shown in the feed.\n     */\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        // Interactions: pull the tip. SafeERC20 reverts if the transfer fails,\n        // so state below only updates on a confirmed transfer.\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        // Effects: counters are stats only, so post-transfer ordering is safe here.\n        tipCount += 1;\n        totalTipped += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw the full collected USDC balance to the owner.\n    function withdraw() external {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n\n        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);\n        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar, awaiting withdrawal.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":65,"oldLines":11,"newStart":65,"newLines":11,"lines":["     function withdraw() external {","         if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();"," ","-        uint256 balance = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));","-        if (balance == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();","+        uint256 collected = usdc.balanceOf(address(this));","+        if (collected == 0) revert NothingToWithdraw();"," ","-        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, balance);","-        emit Withdraw(owner, balance);","+        usdc.safeTransfer(owner, collected);","+        emit Withdraw(owner, collected);","     }"," ","     /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar, awaiting withdrawal."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

\"—\" : `${formatUnits(value, 6)} USDC`);\n\n/**\n * Headline stats for the jar: how many tips, how much has flowed through, the\n * balance waiting to be withdrawn, and an owner-only withdraw button.\n */\nexport const JarStats = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const { data: tipCount } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"tipCount\" });\n  const { data: totalTipped } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"totalTipped\" });\n  const { data: jarBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"balance\" });\n  const { data: owner } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\", functionName: \"owner\" });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: withdraw, isPending: isWithdrawing } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n  });\n\n  const isOwner = !!connectedAddress && !!owner && connectedAddress.toLowerCase() === owner.toLowerCase();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-center\">\n          <div className=\"stat bg-base-200 rounded-box\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Tips</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{tipCount === undefined ? \"—\" : tipCount.toString()}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat bg-base-200 rounded-box\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Total tipped</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(totalTipped)}</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"stat bg-base-200 rounded-box\">\n            <div className=\"stat-title\">Awaiting withdrawal</div>\n            <div className=\"stat-value text-2xl\">{formatUsdc(jarBalance)}</div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n            <span className=\"font-medium\">Jar:</span>\n            {tipJar?.address ? <Address address={tipJar.address} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          {isOwner && (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-outline btn-sm\"\n              disabled={isWithdrawing || !jarBalance || jarBalance === 0n}\n              onClick={async () => {\n                await withdraw({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n              }}\n            >\n              {isWithdrawing ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-xs\" /> : \"Withdraw\"}\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":330,"cache_read_input_tokens":69831,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":330},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"8fde20a0-3e7d-49e4-9d78-6ac65287300e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:16:10.636Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRD9Gxs1UuRsvKDvGp5"}

<Address address={tipJar.address} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" /> : \"—\"}\n          </div>\n          {isOwner && (\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-outline btn-sm\"\n              disabled={isWithdrawing || !jarBalance || jarBalance === 0n}\n              onClick={async () => {\n                await withdraw({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n              }}\n            >\n              {isWithdrawing ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-xs\" /> : \"Withdraw\"}\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMREbu8Yu21CcBNCRjZs","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019xvcybdhn1oMQKSank8q1n","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\n/** Parse a human USDC amount into 6-decimal base units; returns 0n on invalid input. */\nconst parseUsdc = (value: string): bigint => {\n  if (!value || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return 0n;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return 0n;\n  }\n};\n\n/**\n * Form to send a USDC tip. USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a two-step flow:\n * first `approve` the jar to pull the amount, then call `tip`. The button shows\n * whichever step is next and each step has its own pending state.\n */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseUsdc(amount);\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isPending: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isPending: isTipping } = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMRGWdggeE1u3kWeZqgw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01J9NrsosyZarnrdZHR7CAXg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst formatTime = (timestamp?: bigint) => {\n  if (timestamp === undefined) return \"\";\n  return new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString();\n};\n\n/** Live feed of every tip, newest first, read from the on-chain NewTip events. */\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Tip feed</h2>\n\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"flex justify-center py-8\">\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-lg\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : !tips || tips.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-center py-8 opacity-70\">No tips yet — be the first!</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n            {tips.map((tip, index) => (\n              <li key={`${tip.transactionHash}-${index}`} className=\"bg-base-200 rounded-box p-4\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                  <Address address={tip.args.from} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" />\n                  <span className=\"font-bold\">{formatUnits(tip.args.amount ?? 0n, 6)} USDC</span>\n                </div>\n                {tip.args.message && <p className=\"mt-2 break-words\">{tip.args.message}</p>}\n                <p className=\"text-xs opacity-60 mt-1\">{formatTime(tip.args.timestamp)}</p>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2503,"cache_read_input_tokens":72021,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2503},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"dfe05149-7ee6-485c-b35e-6d4e3dd2bcaf","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:16:49.846Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRGTuUjKGjS4DLtekir"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMRLYDJJeKc1jwixe4AN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019HUwWL2vt8sXf6JxnN6B8K","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs","content":"// Node 25+ ships a built-in `localStorage` that is missing the standard WebStorage\n// methods (getItem/setItem/...). During `output: \"export\"` static prerendering,\n// next-themes / RainbowKit call localStorage.getItem() and crash pages (which then\n// 404 on IPFS). Injected into every build process via NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"\n// so it also runs inside Next.js's separate prerender worker (instrumentation.ts does not).\nif (typeof globalThis.localStorage !== \"undefined\" && typeof globalThis.localStorage.getItem !== \"function\") {\n  const store = new Map();\n  globalThis.localStorage = {\n    getItem: key => (store.has(key) ? store.get(key) : null),\n    setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, String(value)),\n    removeItem: key => store.delete(key),\n    clear: () => store.clear(),\n    key: index => [...store.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    get length() {\n      return store.size;\n    },\n  };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":534,"cache_read_input_tokens":79004,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":534},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"b1be78ce-6e5c-4a15-a494-da203644b9b6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:17:38.748Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRLX1NPHVZQEuQbBTwT"}

During `output: \"export\"` static prerendering,\n// next-themes / RainbowKit call localStorage.getItem() and crash pages (which then\n// 404 on IPFS). Injected into every build process via NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"\n// so it also runs inside Next.js's separate prerender worker (instrumentation.ts does not).\nif (typeof globalThis.localStorage !== \"undefined\" && typeof globalThis.localStorage.getItem !== \"function\") {\n  const store = new Map();\n  globalThis.localStorage = {\n    getItem: key => (store.has(key) ? store.get(key) : null),\n    setItem: (key, value) => store.set(key, String(value)),\n    removeItem: key => store.delete(key),\n    clear: () => store.clear(),\n    key: index => [...store.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    get length() {\n      return store.size;\n    },\n  };\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"0562734a-25b7-456f-9d58-294bb6011db7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:19:49.811Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/foundry/Makefile","oldString":"# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337","newString":"# Start a fork. --block-time 1 keeps block.timestamp advancing (needed for the tip feed).\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1","originalFile":".PHONY: build deploy generate-abis get-address account chain compile flatten fork format lint test verify\n\nDEPLOY_SCRIPT ?= script/Deploy.s.sol\n\n# setup wallet for anvil\nsetup-anvil-wallet:\n\tshx rm ~/.foundry/keystores/scaffold-eth-default 2>/dev/null; \tshx rm -rf broadcast/Deploy.s.sol/31337\n\tcast wallet import --private-key 0x2a871d0798f97d79848a013d4936a73bf4cc922c825d33c1cf7073dff6d409c6 --unsafe-password 'localhost' scaffold-eth-default\n\n# Start local chain\nchain: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil\n\n# Start a fork\nfork: setup-anvil-wallet\n\tanvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337\n\n# Deploy the contracts\ndeploy:\n\t@if [ ! -f \"$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)\" ]; then \t\techo \"Error: Deploy script '$(DEPLOY_SCRIPT)' not found\"; \t\texit 1; \tfi\n\t@if [ \"$(RPC_URL)\" = \"localhost\" ]; then \t\tif [ \"$(ETH_KEYSTORE_ACCOUNT)\" = \"scaffold-eth-default\" ]; then \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --password localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\telse \t\t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url localhost --broadcast --ffi; \t\tfi \telse \t\tforge script $(DEPLOY_SCRIPT) --rpc-url $(RPC_URL) --broadcast --ffi; \tfi\n\n# Deploy and generate ABIs\ndeploy-and-generate-abis: deploy generate-abis \n\n# Generate TypeScript ABIs\ngenerate-abis:\n\tnode scripts-js/generateTsAbis.js\n\n# List account\naccount:\n\t@node scripts-js/checkAccountBalance.js\n\n# Get address of a keystore\nget-address:\n\t@cast wallet address --account $(ACCOUNT_NAME)\n\n# Compile contracts\ncompile:\n\tforge compile\n\n# Flatten contracts\nflatten:\n\tforge flatten\n\n# Format code\nformat:\n\tforge fmt && prettier --write ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Lint code\nlint:\n\tforge fmt --check && prettier --check ./scripts-js/**/*.js\n\n# Run tests\ntest:\n\tforge test\n\n# Verify contracts\nverify:\n\tforge script script/VerifyAll.s.sol --ffi --rpc-url $(RPC_URL)\n\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":11,"oldLines":9,"newStart":11,"newLines":9,"lines":[" chain: setup-anvil-wallet","   anvil"," ","-# Start a fork","+# Start a fork. --block-time 1 keeps block.timestamp advancing (needed for the tip feed)."," fork: setup-anvil-wallet","-  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337","+  anvil --fork-url ${FORK_URL} --chain-id 31337 --block-time 1"," "," # Deploy the contracts"," deploy:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

On a\n  // Base fork that keeps eth_getLogs within local blocks — querying from block 0 would\n  // forward to the real Base RPC, which caps getLogs at a 10k block range and errors.\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    watch: true,\n  });","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst formatTime = (timestamp?: bigint) => {\n  if (timestamp === undefined) return \"\";\n  return new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString();\n};\n\n/** Live feed of every tip, newest first, read from the on-chain NewTip events. */\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Tip feed</h2>\n\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"flex justify-center py-8\">\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-lg\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : !tips || tips.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-center py-8 opacity-70\">No tips yet — be the first!</p>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n            {tips.map((tip, index) => (\n              <li key={`${tip.transactionHash}-${index}`} className=\"bg-base-200 rounded-box p-4\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n                  <Address address={tip.args.from} chain={targetNetwork} size=\"sm\" />\n                  <span className=\"font-bold\">{formatUnits(tip.args.amount ?? 0n, 6)} USDC</span>\n                </div>\n                {tip.args.message && <p className=\"mt-2 break-words\">{tip.args.message}</p>}\n                <p className=\"text-xs opacity-60 mt-1\">{formatTime(tip.args.timestamp)}</p>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":10,"newStart":12,"newLines":12,"lines":[" /** Live feed of every tip, newest first, read from the on-chain NewTip events. */"," export const TipFeed = () => {","   const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();","+  // No explicit fromBlock: the hook defaults to the contract's deployedOnBlock. On a","+  // Base fork that keeps eth_getLogs within local blocks — querying from block 0 would","+  // forward to the real Base RPC, which caps getLogs at a 10k block range and errors.","   const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({","     contractName: \"TipJar\",","     eventName: \"NewTip\",","-    fromBlock: 0n,","     watch: true,","   });"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

[])].sort((a, b) => {\n    if (a.blockNumber !== b.blockNumber) return Number(b.blockNumber - a.blockNumber);\n    return Number(b.logIndex) - Number(a.logIndex);\n  });","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { Address } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst formatTime = (timestamp?: bigint) => {\n  if (timestamp === undefined) return \"\";\n  return new Date(Number(timestamp) * 1000).toLocaleString();\n};\n\n/** Live feed of every tip, newest first, read from the on-chain NewTip events. */\nexport const TipFeed = () => {\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  // No explicit fromBlock: the hook defaults to the contract's deployedOnBlock. On a\n  // Base fork that keeps eth_getLogs within local blocks — querying from block 0 would\n  // forward to the real Base RPC, which caps getLogs at a 10k block range and errors.\n  const { data: tips, isLoading } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"TipJar\",\n    eventName: \"NewTip\",\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Tip feed</h2>\n\n        {isLoading ?
