{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KYED3Ehe9NDSSimLJM8Nr4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n2\t\n3\t<h4 align=\"center\">\n4\t  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n5\t  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"d429ea6f-4060-4731-bc00-2a2d7dc62c8e","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:24:16.698Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>","numLines":5,"startLine":1,"totalLines":83}}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMRrRFBki14chNHNst8E","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018i2QvXSyd3Y35L78Ez1ewe","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA tip jar for [Base](https://base.org): send a **USDC** tip with a public message, watch a\nlive feed of tips, and let the jar owner withdraw the collected balance.\n\n- **Contract:** `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` — accepts USDC\n  (`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`) via `approve` + `tip`, emits a `NewTip`\n  event per tip, and lets the owner `withdraw`.\n- **Frontend:** `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` — jar stats, a tip form (approve → tip), and\n  the live tip feed. Ships as a static site for IPFS (see [`DEPLOY.md`](./DEPLOY.md)).\n\nEverything is developed and demoed **locally against real Base state** using an Anvil\nfork, so demo tips move real USDC between test identities with **no real money at risk**.\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Node.js (any recent version for local dev). The IPFS production build is verified on\n  Node 25 thanks to `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`.\n- [Foundry](https://book.getfoundry.sh/getting-started/installation) (`anvil`, `cast`, `forge`).\n- `yarn install` at the repo root.\n\n## Contract tests\n\n```bash\nyarn foundry:test        # or: cd packages/foundry && forge test\n```\n\nTests run against an in-memory 6-decimal mock USDC, so no network/fork is required.\n\n---\n\n## Local workflow — demo against real Base state\n\nThe team demos everything on a local **fork of Base**. The fork is a full copy of Base at\nthe current block, including the real USDC contract and every holder's balance — but it runs\non Anvil (chain id `31337`), so nothing touches mainnet and no real money is spent. We fund a\ncouple of throwaway test identities with real forked USDC and tip between them.\n\nOpen three terminals from the repo root.\n\n### 1. Fork Base\n\n```bash\nyarn fork\n```\n\nThis starts Anvil forking `https://mainnet.base.org` at chain id `31337` with `--block-time 1`\n(so `block.timestamp` keeps advancing and the feed timestamps are live). Leave it running.\n\n> To fork a different endpoint (e.g. a faster Alchemy URL), edit the `fork` script default in\n> `packages/foundry/package.json`.\n\n### 2. Deploy the tip jar to the fork\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy\n```\n\n`DeployTipJar.s.sol` deploys `TipJar` pointed at the canonical Base USDC address (the same\naddress works on the fork and on Base mainnet). The deploy exports the address + ABI to\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer (Anvil account\n`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) is the jar **owner**.\n\n### 3. Fund test identities with real (forked) USDC\n\nTest identities are Anvil's deterministic accounts (already funded with ETH for gas). We give\nthem USDC by **impersonating a real Base USDC whale** on the fork and transferring from it.\n\n```bash\nexport RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545\nexport USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\n\n# A large USDC holder on Base (any USDC-rich address works; find a current one on the\n# Basescan USDC \"Holders\" tab if this balance ever runs low):\nexport WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43\n\n# Two throwaway test identities = Anvil accounts #0 and #1:\nexport ALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266\nexport BOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8\n\n# Impersonate the whale and send each identity 100 USDC (USDC has 6 decimals):\ncast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH for gas\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC\ncast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC\n\n# Verify:\ncast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC      # -> 100000000\n```\n\n### 4. Start the frontend\n\n```bash\nyarn start        # http://localhost:3000\n```\n\n### 5. Tip in the browser\n\n1. Open http://localhost:3000 and **connect** the built-in **Burner Wallet** (shown on the\n   local network), or import an Anvil test key (e.g. Alice's\n   `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`) into your wallet.\n2. Make sure the **address you connect with holds USDC** — fund it in step 3 (for a burner,\n   copy its address from the UI and send USDC to that address instead of Alice/Bob).\n3. Enter an amount + message. Because USDC is an ERC-20 the flow is two steps:\n   **Approve USDC** → **Send tip**. The button shows whichever step is next.\n4. The tip appears in the **Tip feed** and the stats update. Switch to another identity to\n   tip again; connect as the owner to **Withdraw** the collected balance.\n\nYou can also drive the whole flow headlessly with `cast` — see the commands in `DEPLOY.md`\nand the contract functions `tip(uint256,string)` / `withdraw()`.\n\n---\n\n## Deploying to production (static IPFS site)\n\nSee [`DEPLOY.md`](./DEPLOY.md) for the exact build, upload, and post-deploy verification\ncommands. In short: `polyfill-localstorage.cjs` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` produce a\nstatic `out/` directory that you pin to IPFS.\n\n## Project layout\n\n| Path | What |\n| --- | --- |\n| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` | The tip jar contract |\n| `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` | Deploy script (Base USDC address) |\n| `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` | Foundry tests (mock USDC) |\n| `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` | Tip jar page |\n| `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/*` | `JarStats`, `TipForm`, `TipFeed` |\n| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | USDC ABI/address for chains 31337 + 8453 |\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":403,"cache_read_input_tokens":110827,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":403},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"c473af73-9e80-4f11-850d-c805b1fe2ddc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:24:55.458Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRrPvJqfJMnomV3NU1g"}

Open http://localhost:3000 and **connect** the built-in **Burner Wallet** (shown on the\n   local network), or import an Anvil test key (e.g. Alice's\n   `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`) into your wallet.\n2. Leave it running."," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","+> To fork a different endpoint (e.g. a faster Alchemy URL), edit the `fork` script default in","+> `packages/foundry/package.json`."," ","-```","+### 2. Deploy the tip jar to the fork","+","+```bash"," yarn deploy"," ```"," ","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","+`DeployTipJar.s.sol` deploys `TipJar` pointed at the canonical Base USDC address (the same","+address works on the fork and on Base mainnet). The deploy exports the address + ABI to","+`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. The deployer (Anvil account","+`0xa0Ee7A142d267C1f36714E4a8F75612F20a79720`) is the jar **owner**."," ","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","+### 3. Fund test identities with real (forked) USDC"," ","+Test identities are Anvil's deterministic accounts (already funded with ETH for gas). We give","+them USDC by **impersonating a real Base USDC whale** on the fork and transferring from it.","+","+```bash","+export RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8545","+export USDC=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913","+","+# A large USDC holder on Base (any USDC-rich address works; find a current one on the","+# Basescan USDC \"Holders\" tab if this balance ever runs low):","+export WHALE=0xcDAC0d6c6C59727a65F871236188350531885C43","+","+# Two throwaway test identities = Anvil accounts #0 and #1:","+export ALICE=0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266","+export BOB=0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8","+","+# Impersonate the whale and send each identity 100 USDC (USDC has 6 decimals):","+cast rpc anvil_impersonateAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC","+cast rpc anvil_setBalance $WHALE 0xDE0B6B3A7640000 --rpc-url $RPC        # 1 ETH for gas","+cast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $ALICE 100000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC","+cast send $USDC \"transfer(address,uint256)\" $BOB   100000000 --from $WHALE --unlocked --rpc-url $RPC","+cast rpc anvil_stopImpersonatingAccount $WHALE --rpc-url $RPC","+","+# Verify:","+cast call $USDC \"balanceOf(address)(uint256)\" $ALICE --rpc-url $RPC      # -> 100000000"," ```","-yarn start","-```"," ","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+### 4. Start the frontend"," ","-Run smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`","+```bash","+yarn start        # http://localhost:3000","+```"," ","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`","+### 5. Tip in the browser"," ","+1. Open http://localhost:3000 and **connect** the built-in **Burner Wallet** (shown on the","+   local network), or import an Anvil test key (e.g. Alice's","+   `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`) into your wallet.","+2. Make sure the **address you connect with holds USDC** — fund it in step 3 (for a burner,","+   copy its address from the UI and send USDC to that address instead of Alice/Bob).","+3. Enter an amount + message. Because USDC is an ERC-20 the flow is two steps:","+   **Approve USDC** → **Send tip**. The button shows whichever step is next.","+4. The tip appears in the **Tip feed** and the stats update. Switch to another identity to","+   tip again; connect as the owner to **Withdraw** the collected balance."," ","-## Documentation","+You can also drive the whole flow headlessly with `cast` — see the commands in `DEPLOY.md`","+and the contract functions `tip(uint256,string)` / `withdraw()`."," ","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","+---"," ","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","+## Deploying to production (static IPFS site)"," ","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","+See [`DEPLOY.md`](./DEPLOY.md) for the exact build, upload, and post-deploy verification","+commands. In short: `polyfill-localstorage.cjs` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` produce a","+static `out/` directory that you pin to IPFS."," ","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","+## Project layout"," ","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+| Path | What |","+| --- | --- |","+| `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol` | The tip jar contract |","+| `packages/foundry/script/DeployTipJar.s.sol` | Deploy script (Base USDC address) |","+| `packages/foundry/test/TipJar.t.sol` | Foundry tests (mock USDC) |","+| `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx` | Tip jar page |","+| `packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/*` | `JarStats`, `TipForm`, `TipFeed` |","+| `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts` | USDC ABI/address for chains 31337 + 8453 |"]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Foundry, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Foundry. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/foundry/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/foundry/script` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn foundry:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/foundry/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/foundry/script`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

{"type":"rate_limit_event","rate_limit_info":{"status":"allowed_warning","resetsAt":1784937600,"rateLimitType":"five_hour","utilization":0.94,"isUsingOverage":false,"surpassedThreshold":0.9},"uuid":"2b84846e-6206-46b3-9436-29a15fe2ff02","session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMRxHZEtadroDnKaEmRX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now DEPLOY.md:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":734,"cache_read_input_tokens":116867,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":734},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"75de6cfb-cbe3-4354-8687-ceb0afa0b49b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:25:38.267Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRxGXjBVCFqiKgASUfq"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMRxHZEtadroDnKaEmRX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ApZKoEuAMWJ44RBRRLoCKm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/DEPLOY.md","content":"# DEPLOY.md — ship the USDC Tip Jar as a static IPFS site\n\nThe frontend ships as a **static export** pinned to IPFS (no server, no Vercel). This file\nlists the exact commands for the production build, the IPFS upload, and post-deploy\nverification. Run everything from `packages/nextjs` unless noted.\n\n> Local development/demo runs against a **Base fork** (chain `31337`) — see\n> [`README.md`](./README.md). The steps below are for the **real Base mainnet** production\n> site (chain `8453`).\n\n---\n\n## 0. Prerequisites\n\n- `yarn install` at the repo root.\n- The IPFS build is verified on **Node 25** via `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\n  (Node 25 ships a broken global `localStorage` that otherwise crashes static prerendering).\n  Node 20/22 also work.\n- Already configured in this repo, no change needed:\n  - `next.config.ts` — sets `output: \"export\"`, `trailingSlash: true`, `images.unoptimized`\n    when `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true` (trailing slash is required or every route 404s on IPFS).\n  - `scaffold.config.ts` — `pollingInterval: 3000`.\n  - `public/thumbnail.png` — 1200×630 Open Graph image.\n\n## 1. Point the frontend at Base mainnet\n\nFor the local demo the app targets the fork (`chains.foundry`). For production, edit\n`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`:\n\n```ts\n// targetNetworks: [chains.foundry],           // local fork (dev)\ntargetNetworks: [chains.base],                 // production: Base mainnet (chain 8453)\n\nrpcOverrides: {\n  [chains.base.id]: \"https://base-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/<YOUR_ALCHEMY_KEY>\",\n},\n```\n\nUse a dedicated RPC (Alchemy/Infura/BuidlGuidl) — **not** the public `mainnet.base.org` — for\nproduction reliability.\n\n## 2. Deploy the contract to Base mainnet\n\nOnly needed once (or when the contract changes). Requires a funded deployer keystore\n(`yarn account:import` or `yarn generate`). This is the only step that spends real gas; the\ndeployer becomes the jar **owner**.\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base --file DeployTipJar.s.sol\n```\n\nThis writes the Base (`8453`) `TipJar` address + ABI into\n`packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`. USDC is already wired for chain `8453` in\n`externalContracts.ts`.\n\n## 3. Clean production build (static export)\n\n```bash\ncd packages/nextjs\nrm -rf .next out                                   # ALWAYS clean first — avoids shipping a stale build\n\nNEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=\"https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link\" \\\n  NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true \\\n  NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true \\\n  yarn build\n```\n\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL` → your live domain, so OG/Twitter image URLs are absolute\n  (not `localhost`). Set it to the ENS gateway domain you'll use in step 6.\n- `NODE_OPTIONS=--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs` → injected into every build worker so\n  prerendering doesn't crash on Node 25.\n\n## 4. Verify the build BEFORE uploading\n\n```bash\nls out/index.html out/debug/index.html out/blockexplorer/index.html   # each route -> directory + index.html\ngrep -o 'og:image[^>]*content=\"[^\"]*\"' out/index.html                 # absolute https URL, NOT localhost\ngrep -o '<title>[^<]*</title>' out/index.html                         # \"USDC Tip Jar\"\nstat -f '%Sm' app/page.tsx && stat -f '%Sm' out/                      # out/ must be NEWER than source (fresh build)\n```\n\nEvery route must have a `<dir>/index.html` (that's what `trailingSlash: true` produces and\nwhat IPFS gateways resolve). A route missing its `index.html` = it crashed during prerender.\n\n## 5. Upload / pin to IPFS\n\n```bash\nyarn bgipfs upload out\n# Copy the printed CID — e.g. CID: bafybei...\n```\n\n(Or `yarn ipfs` from `packages/nextjs` to build + upload in one step — it already includes the\npolyfill and a clean rebuild; add `NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=...` in front of it so OG URLs\nare absolute.)\n\n**A real code change always yields a new CID.** If the CID didn't change, you uploaded stale\noutput — clean and rebuild (step 3).\n\nPreview the pinned site: `https://community.bgipfs.com/ipfs/<CID>`\n\n## 6. Point ENS at the CID (optional, for a human-readable domain)\n\nTwo mainnet transactions at https://app.ens.domains for `tipjar.yourname.eth`:\n\n1. **New subname** (first deploy only): Subnames → New subname → `tipjar` → confirm.\n2. **Set content hash**: `tipjar.yourname.eth` → Records → Edit → set **Content Hash** to\n   `ipfs://<CID>` → save + confirm.\n\nFor updates, only redo step 2 with the new CID. Prefer the `.eth.link` gateway (better mobile\nsupport): `https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link`.\n\n## 7. Post-deploy verification\n\n```bash\n# a) Home + routes return 200 through the gateway (allow 5–15 min for ENS/IPFS cache):\nfor path in \"\" \"debug/\" \"blockexplorer/\"; do\n  curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}  $path\\n\" -L \"https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link/$path\"\ndone\n\n# b) OG image resolves to the production URL (not localhost):\ncurl -s -L \"https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link\" | grep 'og:image'\n\n# c) ENS content hash matches your CID on-chain:\nRESOLVER=$(cast call 0x00000000000C2e074eC69A0dFb2997BA6C7d2e1e \\\n  \"resolver(bytes32)(address)\" $(cast namehash tipjar.yourname.eth) \\\n  --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com)\ncast call $RESOLVER \"contenthash(bytes32)(bytes)\" \\\n  $(cast namehash tipjar.yourname.eth) --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com\n```\n\nAll routes should return `200` (not `404`), the OG image should be the absolute production URL,\nand the on-chain content hash should decode to `ipfs://<CID>`.\n\n---\n\n### Quick reference\n\n| Step | Command |\n| --- | --- |\n| Deploy contract to Base | `yarn deploy --network base --file DeployTipJar.s.sol` |\n| Clean build (static) | `rm -rf .next out && NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=… NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true yarn build` |\n| Verify output | `ls out/*/index.html && grep og:image out/index.html` |\n| Upload to IPFS | `yarn bgipfs upload out` |\n| Verify live | `curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link/debug/` |\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":734,"cache_read_input_tokens":116867,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":734},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"62062976-cb6a-4f2d-9022-136b4a756b56","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:26:13.006Z","request_id":"req_011CdMRxGXjBVCFqiKgASUfq"}

Post-deploy verification\n\n```bash\n# a) Home + routes return 200 through the gateway (allow 5–15 min for ENS/IPFS cache):\nfor path in \"\" \"debug/\" \"blockexplorer/\"; do\n  curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}  $path\\n\" -L \"https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link/$path\"\ndone\n\n# b) OG image resolves to the production URL (not localhost):\ncurl -s -L \"https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link\" | grep 'og:image'\n\n# c) ENS content hash matches your CID on-chain:\nRESOLVER=$(cast call 0x00000000000C2e074eC69A0dFb2997BA6C7d2e1e \\\n  \"resolver(bytes32)(address)\" $(cast namehash tipjar.yourname.eth) \\\n  --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com)\ncast call $RESOLVER \"contenthash(bytes32)(bytes)\" \\\n  $(cast namehash tipjar.yourname.eth) --rpc-url https://eth.llamarpc.com\n```\n\nAll routes should return `200` (not `404`), the OG image should be the absolute production URL,\nand the on-chain content hash should decode to `ipfs://<CID>`.\n\n---\n\n### Quick reference\n\n| Step | Command |\n| --- | --- |\n| Deploy contract to Base | `yarn deploy --network base --file DeployTipJar.s.sol` |\n| Clean build (static) | `rm -rf .next out && NEXT_PUBLIC_PRODUCTION_URL=… NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\" NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true NEXT_PUBLIC_IGNORE_BUILD_ERROR=true yarn build` |\n| Verify output | `ls out/*/index.html && grep og:image out/index.html` |\n| Upload to IPFS | `yarn bgipfs upload out` |\n| Verify live | `curl -s -o /dev/null -w \"%{http_code}\" -L https://tipjar.yourname.eth.link/debug/` |\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"72165acc-91aa-464b-88de-19397a3d65a8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:28:15.183Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/dev/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-001/2026-07-24T190908Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/tipjar/TipForm.tsx","oldString":"  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > allowance;","newString":"  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n  // Treat an unknown (still-loading) allowance as 0 so we never show \"Send tip\" before the\n  // jar is actually approved — that would just revert.\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount > (allowance ?? 0n);","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\n/** Parse a human USDC amount into 6-decimal base units; returns 0n on invalid input. */\nconst parseUsdc = (value: string): bigint => {\n  if (!value || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return 0n;\n  try {\n    return parseUnits(value, USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    return 0n;\n  }\n};\n\n/**\n * Form to send a USDC tip. USDC is an ERC-20, so tipping is a two-step flow:\n * first `approve` the jar to pull the amount, then call `tip`. The button shows\n * whichever step is next and each step has its own pending state.\n */\nexport const TipForm = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { data: tipJar } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const parsedAmount = parseUsdc(amount);\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, tipJar?.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isPending: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeTipJar, isPending: isTipping } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"TipJar\" });\n\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > allowance;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!tipJar?.address) return;\n    await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [tipJar.address, parsedAmount] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  const handleTip = async () => {\n    await writeTipJar({ functionName: \"tip\", args: [parsedAmount, message] });\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMessage(\"\");\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-xl w-full\">\n      <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"card-title\">Send a tip</h2>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Amount (USDC)</span>\n            <span className=\"label-text-alt\">\n              Balance: {usdcBalance === undefined ? \"—\" : formatUnits(usdcBalance, USDC_DECIMALS)}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            placeholder=\"5.00\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"form-control w-full\">\n          <div className=\"label\">\n            <span className=\"label-text\">Message</span>\n          </div>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            maxLength={140}\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for building!\"\n            className=\"input input-bordered w-full\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        {insufficientBalance && <p className=\"text-error text-sm\">Amount exceeds your USDC balance.</p>}\n\n        {!connectedAddress ? (\n          <button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled>\n            Connect a wallet to tip\n          </button>\n        ) : needsApproval ? (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!hasAmount || insufficientBalance || isApproving}\n            onClick={handleApprove}\n          >\n            {isApproving ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Approve USDC\"}\n          </button>\n        ) : (\n          <button\n            className=\"btn btn-primary\"\n            disabled={!hasAmount || insufficientBalance || isTipping}\n            onClick={handleTip}\n          >\n            {isTipping ? <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> : \"Send tip\"}\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":48,"oldLines":7,"newStart":48,"newLines":9,"lines":[" ","   const hasAmount = parsedAmount > 0n;","   const insufficientBalance = usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance;","-  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > allowance;","+  // Treat an unknown (still-loading) allowance as 0 so we never show \"Send tip\" before the","+  // jar is actually approved — that would just revert.","+  const needsApproval = parsedAmount > (allowance ?? 0n);"," ","   const handleApprove = async () => {","     if (!tipJar?.address) return;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016c1YRaY9Hc9x7ETPBMStF4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: se2-node-static-export\n3\tdescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\n4\tmetadata: \n5\t  node_type: memory\n6\t  type: project\n7\t  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n8\t  modified: 2026-07-24T17:18:54.287Z\n9\t---\n10\t\n11\tIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n12\t(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\n13\tpage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n14\t\n15\t**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\n16\tobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n17\t\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\n18\tglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n19\t\n20\t**Fix A (Node 20/22):** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is\n21\tinstalled and verified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into\n22\tNODE_OPTIONS — that flag errors on Node <22.4.\n23\t\n24\t**Fix B (works on Node 25, verified):** ship a `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\n25\tthat reinstalls the WebStorage methods when a global `localStorage` exists but\n26\tlacks `getItem`, and inject it with `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"`.\n27\tMust use `--require` (not `instrumentation.ts`) so it loads in every prerender\n28\tworker. Add the same NODE_OPTIONS to the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts too —\n29\totherwise `next dev` SSR throws the same error on Node 25 (page still hydrates\n30\tclient-side, but client components fail to SSR). Polyfill is a no-op on Node 20/22.\n31\t\n32\tAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n33\t(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\n34\tit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\n35\tnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n36\t\n37\tWorkaround that DOES let a build-time env ternary switch networks (verified,\n38\ttip-jar goal): `targetNetworks = isIpfs ? ([chains.base] as const) : ([chains.foundry] as const)`\n39\tkeeps each branch's chain-id literal, so `ContractName` stays valid AS LONG AS both\n40\tchains declare the same contract names (put shared contracts under both chain ids in\n41\texternalContracts). Two gotchas: (1) do NOT annotate it `readonly [chains.Chain]` —\n42\tthat widens `.id` to `number` and breaks `ContractName`. (2) The union breaks wagmi's\n43\t`chains` type in `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx`; fix by annotating\n44\t`export const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = ...`. Also note\n45\t`deepMergeContracts` lets externalContracts OVERRIDE deployedContracts for the same\n46\tname+chain, so an env-driven external address wins over a generated one.\n47\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"7173b7a8-d32d-4b44-a079-7e16a01c50b5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:28:38.206Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/spencerfaber/.claude/projects/-Users-spencerfaber-dev-ethskills-evals/memory/se2-node-static-export.md","content":"---\nname: se2-node-static-export\ndescription: Scaffold-ETH 2 Next.js static export (IPFS build) crashes on Node >=23 due to a global localStorage\nmetadata: \n  node_type: memory\n  type: project\n  originSessionId: 2ee6c09f-05f4-414a-b582-02feea8e809f\n  modified: 2026-07-24T17:18:54.287Z\n---\n\nIn the ethskills-evals SE-2 workspaces, `NEXT_PUBLIC_IPFS_BUILD=true yarn next:build`\n(Next.js static `output: \"export\"`) fails prerendering the stock `/_not-found`\npage with `TypeError: localStorage.getItem is not a function`.\n\n**Cause:** Node >=23 (env default here is 25.9.0) exposes a global `localStorage`\nobject; wagmi/burner-connector code that guards with `typeof localStorage !==\n\"undefined\"` then runs during SSR prerender and blows up. Node 20/22 have no\nglobal `localStorage`, so the guard works.\n\n**Fix A (Node 20/22):** build with Node 20 or 22 LTS (`nvm use`, e.g. 22.2.0 is\ninstalled and verified). Do NOT bake `--no-experimental-webstorage` into\nNODE_OPTIONS — that flag errors on Node <22.4.\n\n**Fix B (works on Node 25, verified):** ship a `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs`\nthat reinstalls the WebStorage methods when a global `localStorage` exists but\nlacks `getItem`, and inject it with `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ./polyfill-localstorage.cjs\"`.\nMust use `--require` (not `instrumentation.ts`) so it loads in every prerender\nworker. Add the same NODE_OPTIONS to the nextjs `dev`/`start` scripts too —\notherwise `next dev` SSR throws the same error on Node 25 (page still hydrates\nclient-side, but client components fail to SSR). Polyfill is a no-op on Node 20/22.\n\nAlso: `scaffold.config.ts` `targetNetworks` must be a single concrete network\n(compile-time value the contract types derive from). A runtime env ternary makes\nit a union type and collapses `ContractName` to the intersection of both\nnetworks' contracts, breaking type-check.\n\nWorkaround that DOES let a build-time env ternary switch networks (verified,\ntip-jar goal): `targetNetworks = isIpfs ? ([chains.base] as const) : ([chains.foundry] as const)`\nkeeps each branch's chain-id literal, so `ContractName` stays valid AS LONG AS both\nchains declare the same contract names (put shared contracts under both chain ids in\nexternalContracts). Two gotchas: (1) do NOT annotate it `readonly [chains.Chain]` —\nthat widens `.id` to `number` and breaks `ContractName`. (2) The union breaks wagmi's\n`chains` type in `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx`; fix by annotating\n`export const enabledChains: readonly [Chain, ...Chain[]] = ...`. Also note\n`deepMergeContracts` lets externalContracts OVERRIDE deployedContracts for the same\nname+chain, so an env-driven external address wins over a generated one.\n","numLines":47,"startLine":1,"totalLines":47}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdMSG26p6V5yMMbn3umpz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is built and validated. Here's the summary.\n\n## USDC Tip Jar for Base — done\n\nA USDC tip jar contract + a tip page (feed + form), developed against a **local Base fork** so demo tips move real USDC between test identities with no money at risk, and configured to ship as a **static IPFS build**.\n\n### What was built\n- **Contract** — `packages/foundry/contracts/TipJar.sol`: pulls USDC via `approve` + `safeTransferFrom`, emits a `NewTip(from, amount, message, timestamp)` event per tip (the feed source), tracks `tipCount`/`totalTipped`, owner-only `withdraw`. Deploy script uses the canonical Base USDC address (same on fork and mainnet).\n- **Tests** — `TipJar.t.sol` (11 tests, incl. a fuzz test) against a mock 6-decimal USDC so `forge test` runs offline. All pass.\n- **Frontend** — `app/page.tsx` + `components/tipjar/{JarStats,TipForm,TipFeed}.tsx`: stats, a two-step **Approve USDC → Send tip** form (separate pending states), and a live feed. USDC ERC-20 wired into `externalContracts.ts` for chains 31337 (fork) and 8453 (Base).\n- **Docs** — `README.md` (exact local fork workflow) and `DEPLOY.md` (build/upload/verify commands).\n\n### Validated end-to-end\n- `forge test` → 11/11 pass; `tsc` clean.\n- **Local fork demo**: forked Base (`yarn fork`), deployed TipJar, impersonated a real USDC whale to fund two Anvil test identities with 100 USDC each, tipped 5 + 10 USDC from both → jar held 15 USDC, `tipCount=2`, owner `withdraw` returned 15 USDC. `NewTip` events query correctly.\n- **Static IPFS build**: produces trailing-slash routes (`/`, `/debug/`, `/blockexplorer/`), absolute OG URL (not localhost), and a custom 1200×630 thumbnail. Verified it compiles for **both** the foundry (local) and base (production) targets.\n- `yarn start` renders the page (HTTP 200) with the full UI.\n\n### Two Node-25 fixes that were required\nNode 25 ships a broken global `localStorage`. I added `packages/nextjs/polyfill-localstorage.cjs` and wired `NODE_OPTIONS=\"--require ...\"` into the IPFS build **and** the `dev`/`start` scripts — without it, both the static prerender *and* `next dev` SSR crash with `localStorage.getItem is not a function`. Also made `yarn fork` default to the Base RPC (the stock `mainnet` alias pointed at a dead host) with `--block-time 1` for live feed timestamps.\n\nPer the task, nothing was uploaded — the deliverables are the working local setup, the production build config, and DEPLOY.md. The committed `scaffold.config.ts` targets the fork for the local demo; DEPLOY.md documents the one-line switch to Base for production."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":374,"cache_read_input_tokens":134801,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":374,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ea553ab1-8cde-4fed-9758-7abfb76cf0a7","uuid":"5426d36a-4db4-4d8b-a3a0-3e62bc44ecb8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T19:29:52.268Z","request_id":"req_011CdMSFzoBcB6hVz8mX1xSS"}
